<div id="crazy-8s-the-soloversal-blueprint">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title"><span style="color: #0000ff;">THE SOLOVERSAL BLUEPRINT [x1]:</span></h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">The Architect of the Soloverse Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Architect Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Conceptual Targeting</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Counterable</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Persistent</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/19-the-soloversal-blueprint.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Activate before You Start a <a href="#glossary:playable-turn">Playable Turn</span></strong></a>.</li>
<li>To use this Card, Flip it Face-Up on the table in front of You [as proof of ownership], and resolve it's Effects.</li>
<li>This Effect lasts for the rest of the game, or until it is Deactivated or Overwritten.
<ul>
<li>You May Deactivate this Card before you start any <a href="#glossary:playable-turn">Playable Turn</span></strong></a>. To Deactivate this Card, Simply Remove it from the Game.</li>
<li>Should a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect be Activated, it will Overwrite this one, Removing it from the Game.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="a">
<li>For Yourself:</span></strong> Pure Chaos Cards become any Chaos Card in the Game You want when Played for the Rest of the Game.</li>
<li>Once per Game for each other Player:</span></strong> Any Chaos Card Played by any other Player is Played as a Pure Chaos Card for the that Turn.</li> 
</ol>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Can only be <a href="#glossary:counter">Countered</span></strong></a> <strong>*** At the Moment of Activation ***</strong> by the Architect Chaos Card: <a href="#crazy-8s-unstable-soloversal-paradox">Unstable Soloversal Paradox</a>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be used as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
<li>Only One Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect Can be Active at the same time.</li>
<li>Activating a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect will Overwrite the Previous One.</li>
<li>You Cannot Go Out with this Card Activated.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-architectural-glitch">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title"><span style="color: #8cfffb;">ARCHITECTURAL GLITCH [x1]:</span></h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">The Architect of the Soloverse Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Architect Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Conceptual Targeting</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Counterable</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Persistent</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/19-architectural-glitch.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Activate before You Start a <a href="#glossary:playable-turn">Playable Turn</span></strong></a>.</li>
<li>To use this Card, Flip it Face-Up on the table in front of You [as proof of ownership], and resolve it's Effects.</li>
<li>This Effect lasts for the rest of the game, or until it is Deactivated or Overwritten.
<ul>
<li>You May Deactivate this Card before you start any <a href="#glossary:playable-turn">Playable Turn</span></strong></a>. To Deactivate this Card, Simply Remove it from the Game.</li>
<li>Should a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect be Activated, it will Overwrite this one, Removing it from the Game.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>All <a href="#glossary:counter">Counter Effect</span></strong></a> Chaos Cards Cannot be Played for the Rest of the Game.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Can only be <a href="#glossary:counter">Countered</span></strong></a> <strong>*** At the Moment of Activation ***</strong> by the Architect Chaos Card: <a href="#crazy-8s-unstable-soloversal-paradox">Unstable Soloversal Paradox</span></strong></a>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be used as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Only One Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect Can be Active at the same time.</li>
<li>Activating a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect will Overwrite the Previous One.</li>
<li>You Cannot Go Out with a Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Activated.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">

</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-the-soloverse-loophole">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title"><span style="color: #aded8f;">THE SOLOVERSE LOOPHOLE [x1]:</span></h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">The Architect of the Soloverse Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Architect Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Counterable</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Conceptual Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/19-the-soloverse-loophole.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Activate before You Start a <a href="#glossary:playable-turn">Playable Turn</span></strong></a>.</li>
<li>This Card Can be Used Two Times in Any Game.</li>
<li>The First Time You Use this Card, Flip it Face-Up on the table in front of You [as proof of ownership], and resolve it's Effects.</li>
<li>This Second Time You Use this Card, resolve it's Effects then Remove this Card from the Game.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>Declare a Number [0-9].</li>
<li>Discard the Top card of the Deck until a Number Card [0-9] is Revealed.</li>
<li>The Number that is Revealed determines one of the following Effects:
<ol type="a">
<li>Any Other Number:</span></strong> The Number of Cards all other Players <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a>.</li>
<li>The Number You Declared:</span></strong> You Win Instantly.</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Can be <a href="#glossary:counter">Countered</span></strong></a> <strong>*** At the Moment of Activation or the Moment of it's Special Victory Condition Activating***</strong> by the Architect Chaos Card: <a href="#crazy-8s-unstable-soloversal-paradox">Unstable Soloversal Paradox</span></strong></a>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be used as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>

<div id="crazy-8s-the-architects-source-code">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title"><span style="color: #0ed145;">THE ARCHITECT'S SOURCE CODE [x1]:</span></h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">The Architect of the Soloverse Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Architect Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Conceptual Targeting</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Counterable</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Persistent</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/19-the-architects-source-code.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Activate before You Start a <a href="#glossary:playable-turn">Playable Turn</span></strong></a>.</li>
<li>To use this Card, Flip it Face-Up on the table in front of You [as proof of ownership], and resolve it's Effects.</li>
<li>This Effect lasts for the rest of the game, or until it is Deactivated or Overwritten.
<ul>
<li>You May Deactivate this Card before you start any <a href="#glossary:playable-turn">Playable Turn</span></strong></a>. To Deactivate this Card, Simply Remove it from the Game.</li>
<li>Should a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect be Activated, it will Overwrite this one, Removing it from the Game.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>Change any 1 Rule in the Game, no Exceptions, for the Rest of the Game.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLES:</strong>
<ol>
<li>You Can change the Victory Condition to something like:
<ol type="a">
<li>All Players Must have 1 Card instead in Their Hand to Win.</li>
<li>All Players Must have 20 Cards instead in Their Hand to Win.</li>
<li>All Players Must Discard more than 7 Cards from Their Hand at any time to Win.</li>
<li>Prevent a Certain Player from Winning, regardless of existing Victory Conditions. They are not removed from the Game, They Must still Play and <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> a Card if They Cannot Play one.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>You could Prevent Certain Numbered Cards, a Certain Coloured Set of Cards or even Certain Classes of Cards from being Played.</li>
<li>What about forcing Players to <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> a Card before Starting Every Turn?</li>
<li>Now you have the Source Code of the Soloverse at Your Fingertips, You have Unlimited Access to change the Game's Rules as You see fit.</li>
<li>No Restrictions in What you Can Change in the Game's Rules, except for This Card's Rules, They Must all be Followed.</li>
</ol>
</div>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Can only be <a href="#glossary:counter">Countered</span></strong></a> <strong>*** At the Moment of Activation ***</strong> by the Architect Chaos Card: <a href="#crazy-8s-unstable-soloversal-paradox">Unstable Soloversal Paradox</span></strong></a>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be used as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>UNIQUE RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>The Architect's Source Code Cannot change it's own Rules.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Only One Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect Can be Active at the same time.</li>
<li>Activating a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect will Overwrite the Previous One.</li>
<li>You Cannot Go Out with a Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Activated.</li>
</ul>
</div>

<ul>
<li>You May Deactivate this Card before you start any <a href="#glossary:playable-turn">Playable Turn</span></strong></a>. To Deactivate this Card, Simply Remove it from the Game.</li>
<li>Should a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect be Activated, it will Overwrite this one, Removing it from the Game.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>Any <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw Effects</span></strong></a> <a href="#glossary:targeted">Targeting</a> You will automatically bypass Your Turn completely.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Can only be <a href="#glossary:counter">Countered</span></strong></a> <strong>*** At the Moment of Activation ***</strong> by the Architect Chaos Card: <a href="#crazy-8s-unstable-soloversal-paradox">Unstable Soloversal Paradox</span></strong></a>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be used as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Only One Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect Can be Active at the same time.</li>
<li>Activating a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect will Overwrite the Previous One.</li>
<li>You Cannot Go Out with a Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Activated.</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title"><span style="color: #9d00ff;">SOLOVERSAL SINGULARITY [x1]:</span></h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">The Architect of the Soloverse Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Offensive & Architect Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Counterable</span></strong></li>

<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Conceptual Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/19-soloversal-singularity.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Activate before You Start a <a href="#glossary:playable-turn">Playable Turn</span></strong></a>.</li>
<li>This Card Can be Used Two Times in Any Game.</li>
<li>The First Time You Use this Card, Flip it Face-Up on the table in front of You [as proof of ownership], and resolve it's Effects.</li>
<li>This Second Time You Use this Card, resolve it's Effects then Remove this Card from the Game.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>For the next Round of Turns, all Players give You Their Cards.</li>
<li>You get to choose what Cards each Player Plays for Their Turn.</li>
<li>This includes not Playing Cards at all, forcing them to <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> instead and wasting any potentially powerful Chaos Cards early.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be used as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Only One Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect Can be Active at the same time.</li>
<li>Activating a Second Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Effect will Overwrite the Previous One.</li>
<li>You Cannot Go Out with a Persistent Architect Class Chaos Card Activated.</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-repetition-stone">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title"><span style="color: #0000ff;">REPETITION STONE [x1]:</span></h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Cataclysm Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Offensive & Ancient Stone Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Next & Specific Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/16-cataclysm-expansion-1of-6-repetition-stone.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>To use an Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, Turn it Sideways.</li>
<li>Each Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card’s Effect Can only be used Once Each Turn; however, You Can use it on additional Turns afterwards.</li>
<li>Use of Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects do NOT count towards Your one Chaos Card per Turn Restriction.</li>
<li>Activate after any Player's Turn.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
 <ol type="a">
<li>Once between Your PLAYABLE TURNS, force one other Player to take Their Turn again.</li>
<li>After any of Your PLAYABLE TURNS, decide if You will take Your Turn again or not.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="note-box">
<strong>NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If the <a href="#glossary:targeted">Targeted</span></strong></a> Player [including Yourself] faced Action Class or Chaos Card Effects that Turn, they MUST face them again.</li>
<li>If the Card they faced last time was a Counter Class Chaos Card, redirect to the immediately previous Chaos Card’s Effect instead.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>UNIQUE RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Effect only applies to the last actionable Card played, NOT the total of <a href="#glossary:stackable">Stacked</a> Effects [if any].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>All Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects Cannot be <a href="#glossary:counter">Countered</span></strong></a>, Including Their Special Victory Condition.</li>
<li>Only 1 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Can be used each Turn [compare BULK POSSESSION NOTES].</li>
<li>All Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects Cannot affect the same Players two Activations in a row, regardless of who is the User and who is the <a href="#glossary:targeted">Target</a>.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If someone uses the Teleportation Stone to take control of Your seat and Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards, the Teleportation Stone Cannot reclaim them. You Must use the Teleportation Stone to <a href="#glossary:targeted">Target</a> another Player.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Individual Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards Can only <a href="#glossary:targeted">Target</a> the other Player every second Turn.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="compatibility-box">
<strong>COMPATIBILITY NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards May NOT count towards Your Hand’s Card count [so You Can still win with no Cards even if You possess one], but they Can still be stolen via card Swapping means; however, if doing this, You Can only Swap Hand Cards or Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If chosen, Paradigm Shift’s Group Targeting Swap Effect Can <a href="#glossary:targeted">Target</a> all Player’s Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards instead of Their Hand Cards [the Swap includes All Players Except Previous Unrestriction Stone Users, regardless of whether they currently own an Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card or not].</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>Furthermore, Hand Discarding Effects affect both Hand and Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. However, Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards are collected, Shuffled and <a href="#glossary:deal">Re-Dealt</a> according to Their Setup Rules.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Card Effects like Black Hole.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>BULK POSSESSION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>2 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 3 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES NOT include the ability to play Another Chaos Card or Ancient Stone.</li>
<li><strong>3 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 4 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES include the ability to play another Chaos Card but DOES NOT include the ability to play another Ancient Stone.</li>
<li><strong>4 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 5 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES include the ability to play another Chaos Card AND another Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card.</li>
<li><strong>5 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>User:</strong>
<ol type="a">
<li>At any point during another Player’s Turn, Attempt to take possession of the last Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card. This Must be Attempted Once, and only Once, between Your PLAYABLE TURNS.</li>
<li>Failure to Attempt to do so or being prevented from doing so will result in all Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards being Collected and <a href="#glossary:deal">Re-Dealt</a> according to Their Setup Rules.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li><strong>The Last Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Holder:</strong>
<ol type="a">
<li>If the User attempts to take possession of Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, Slap the Table to Defend Yourself.</li>
<li>If You Slap the Table and the User did NOT attempt to take possession of Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, You Forfeit it to them.</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="special-victory-condition-note-box">
<strong>SPECICAL VICTORY CONDITION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>6 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> Possessing All 6 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards at once instantly Wins You the game [regardless of the number of Cards You possess; or whose Turn it actually is].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="note-box">
<strong>NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Any time a Player is Removed from or Restored to the Game by any means, Collect all Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards, Shuffle and <a href="#glossary:deal">Re-Deal</a> them to all Players still currently In the Game. But You Must maintain Play Order [as in the Player that caused this Removal/Restoration Effect (either directly or indirectly) Must perform the above Shuffle & <a href="#glossary:deal">Re-Deal</a> of the Ancient Stones; and as the Dealer, they are last to receive one].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Special Victory Condition now Activates with by possessing all 4 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards in Play [Compare <a href="/games/01-a-crazy-8s-game-rulebook" class="matrix-link">(01-A) Soloverse 01-A Crazy 8's Game Rulebook: Cataclysm Expansion Additional Setup Rules</a>].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">

<ul>
<li>Simply Swap Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards.</li>

</ul>
</div>
<div class="compatibility-box">

<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>

</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>Furthermore, Hand Discarding Effects affect both Hand and Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. However, Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards are collected, Shuffled and <a href="#glossary:deal">Re-Dealt</a> according to Their Setup Rules.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Card Effects like Black Hole.</li>

</div>
</li>

</div>
<div class="advanced-box">

</li>

<div id="crazy-8s-illumination-stone">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title"><span style="color: #fff200;">ILLUMINATION STONE [x1]:</span></h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Cataclysm Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Ancient Stone Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Group Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/16-cataclysm-expansion-5-of-6-illumination-stone.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>To use an Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, Turn it Sideways.</li>
<li>Each Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card’s Effect Can only be used Once Each Turn; however, You Can use it on additional Turns afterwards.</li>
<li>Use of Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects do NOT count towards Your one Chaos Card per Turn Restriction.</li>
<li>Activate before You start a <a href="#glossary:playable-turn">Playable Turn</span></strong></a>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li><a href="#glossary:deal">Deal</a> 1 Card to Yourself.</li>
<li>Force all Players to set all but 1 Card in Their Handas Face-Up on the table in front of them.
<div class="note-box">
<strong>NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Everyone Can keep 1 Card secret.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="tip-box">
<strong>TIP:</strong>
<ul>
<li>While You Can keep 1 Card Secret, it doesn’t have to be Your best Card; in effect You could bluff the other Players into thinking You have something better.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>This Effect lasts until the start of Your next <a href="#glossary:playable-turn">Playable Turn</span></strong></a>.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="compatibility-note">
<ul>
<li>There is nothing wrong in using Chaos Cards with a Card Swapping Effect to take another Player’s Secret Card in exchange for any Card of Your own.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>UNIQUE RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>You Must show Your own Hand as well.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>All Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects Cannot be <a href="#glossary:counter">Countered</span></strong></a>, Including Their Special Victory Condition.</li>
<li>Only 1 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Can be used each Turn [compare BULK POSSESSION NOTES].</li>
<li>All Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects Cannot affect the same Players two Activations in a row, regardless of who is the User and who is the <a href="#glossary:targeted">Target</a>.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If someone uses the Teleportation Stone to take control of Your seat and Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards, the Teleportation Stone Cannot reclaim them. You Must use the Teleportation Stone to <a href="#glossary:targeted">Target</a> another Player.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Individual Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards Can only <a href="#glossary:targeted">Target</a> the other Player every second Turn.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="compatibility-box">
<strong>COMPATIBILITY NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards May NOT count towards Your Hand’s Card count [so You Can still win with no Cards even if You possess one], but they Can still be stolen via card Swapping means; however, if doing this, You Can only Swap Hand Cards or Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If chosen, Paradigm Shift’s Group Targeting Swap Effect Can <a href="#glossary:targeted">Target</a> all Player’s Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards instead of Their Hand Cards [the Swap includes All Players Except Previous Unrestriction Stone Users, regardless of whether they currently own an Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card or not].</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>Furthermore, Hand Discarding Effects affect both Hand and Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. However, Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards are collected, Shuffled and <a href="#glossary:deal">Re-Dealt</a> according to Their Setup Rules.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Card Effects like Black Hole.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>BULK POSSESSION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>2 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 3 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES NOT include the ability to play Another Chaos Card or Ancient Stone.</li>
<li><strong>3 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 4 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES include the ability to play another Chaos Card but DOES NOT include the ability to play another Ancient Stone.</li>
<li><strong>4 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 5 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES include the ability to play another Chaos Card AND another Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card.</li>
<li><strong>5 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>User:</strong>
<ol type="a">
<li>At any point during another Player’s Turn, Attempt to take possession of the last Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card. This Must be Attempted Once, and only Once, between Your PLAYABLE TURNS.</li>
<li>Failure to Attempt to do so or being prevented from doing so will result in all Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards being Collected and <a href="#glossary:deal">Re-Dealt</a> according to Their Setup Rules.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li><strong>The Last Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Holder:</strong>
<ol type="a">
<li>IF the User attempts to take possession of Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, Slap the Table to Defend Yourself.</li>
<li>IF You Slap the Table and the User did NOT attempt to take possession of Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, You Forfeit it to them.</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="special-victory-condition-note-box">
<strong>SPECICAL VICTORY CONDITION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>6 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> Possessing All 6 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards at once instantly Wins You the game [regardless of the number of Cards You possess; or whose Turn it actually is].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="note-box">
<strong>NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>NOTES: Any time a Player is Removed from or Restored to the Game by any means, Collect all Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards, Shuffle and <a href="#glossary:deal">Re-Deal</a> them to all Players still currently In the Game. But You Must maintain Play Order [as in the Player that caused this Removal/Restoration Effect (either directly or indirectly) Must perform the above Shuffle & <a href="#glossary:deal">Re-Deal</a> of the Ancient Stones; and as the Dealer, they are last to receive one].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Special Victory Condition now Activates with by possessing all 4 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards in Play [Compare <a href="/01-a-crazy-8s-game-rulebook" class="matrix-link">(01-A) Soloverse 01-A Crazy 8's Game Rulebook: Cataclysm Expansion Additional Setup Rules</a>].</li>
</ul>
</div>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-unrestriction-stone">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title"><span style="color: #ff7f27;">UNRESTRICTION STONE [x1]:</span></h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Cataclysm Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Ancient Stone Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Restriction Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/16-cataclysm-expansion-6-of-6-unrestriction-stone.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>To use an Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, Turn it Sideways.</li>
<li>Each Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card’s Effect Can only be used Once Each Turn; however, You Can use it on additional Turns afterwards.</li>
<li>Use of Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects do NOT count towards Your one Chaos Card per Turn Restriction.</li>
<li>Activate before You start a <a href="#glossary:playable-turn">Playable Turn</span></strong></a>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ul>
<li>When activated, the User Can choose to disable the Restrictions of all Their Chaos Cards [and I mean all Restrictions of all Chaos Cards; so, remember to consider the “Class Restrictions” NOT just the individual “Card Restrictions”].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COMMUTATION OF THE ANCIENTS EFFECT:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If at the end of Your Turn You chose to use the above ability, this Effect activates:
<ol>
<li>First, collect all Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards and return them to the Cataclysm Side Deck and Shuffle it.</li>
<li>Next, Remove 1 Player of Your Choice from the Game.</li>
<li>Then, if more than one Player has previously been Removed from the Game by any means, You Must choose one to Restore to the Game.</li>
<li><a href="#glossary:deal">Re-Deal</a> all Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards to all Players now currently in the Game, but You Must maintain Play Order [See: RESTRICTION below].</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>UNIQUE RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>The Unrestriction Stone Cannot disable its own Restrictions.</li>
<li>You Cannot receive any more Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards again for the rest of the game.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>All Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects Cannot be <a href="#glossary:counter">Countered</span></strong></a>, Including Their Special Victory Condition.</li>
<li>Only 1 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Can be used each Turn [compare BULK POSSESSION NOTES].</li>
<li>All Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Effects Cannot affect the same Players two Activations in a row, regardless of who is the User and who is the <a href="#glossary:targeted">Target</a>.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If someone uses the Teleportation Stone to take control of Your seat and Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards, the Teleportation Stone Cannot reclaim them. You Must use the Teleportation Stone to <a href="#glossary:targeted">Target</a> another Player.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Individual Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards Can only <a href="#glossary:targeted">Target</a> the other Player every second Turn.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="compatibility-box">
<strong>COMPATIBILITY NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards May NOT count towards Your Hand’s Card count [so You Can still win with no Cards even if You possess one], but they Can still be stolen via card Swapping means; however, if doing this, You Can only Swap Hand Cards or Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If chosen, Paradigm Shift’s Group Targeting Swap Effect Can <a href="#glossary:targeted">Target</a> all Player’s Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards instead of Their Hand Cards [the Swap includes All Players Except Previous Unrestriction Stone Users, regardless of whether they currently own an Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card or not].</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>Furthermore, Hand Discarding Effects affect both Hand and Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. However, Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards are collected, Shuffled and <a href="#glossary:deal">Re-Dealt</a> according to Their Setup Rules.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Card Effects like Black Hole.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>BULK POSSESSION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>2 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 3 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES NOT include the ability to play Another Chaos Card or Ancient Stone.</li>
<li><strong>3 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 4 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES include the ability to play another Chaos Card but DOES NOT include the ability to play another Ancient Stone.</li>
<li><strong>4 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> You Can choose to have a second Turn every Turn You possess a total of 5 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards. This Turn DOES include the ability to play another Chaos Card AND another Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card.</li>
<li><strong>5 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>User:</strong>
<ol type="a">
<li>At any point during another Player’s Turn, Attempt to take possession of the last Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card. This Must be Attempted Once, and only Once, between Your PLAYABLE TURNS.</li>
<li>Failure to Attempt to do so or being prevented from doing so will result in all Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards being Collected and <a href="#glossary:deal">Re-Dealt</a> according to Their Setup Rules.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li><strong>The Last Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card Holder:</strong>
<ol type="a">
<li>IF the User attempts to take possession of Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, Slap the Table to Defend Yourself.</li>
<li>IF You Slap the Table and the User did NOT attempt to take possession of Your Ancient Stone Class Chaos Card, You Forfeit it to them.</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="special-victory-condition-note-box">
<strong>SPECICAL VICTORY CONDITION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li><strong>6 ANCIENT STONE CLASS CHAOS CARDS:</strong> Possessing All 6 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards at once instantly Wins You the game [regardless of the number of Cards You possess; or whose Turn it actually is].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="note-box">
<strong>NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Any time a Player is Removed from or Restored to the Game by any means, Collect all Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards, Shuffle and <a href="#glossary:deal">Re-Deal</a> them to all Players still currently In the Game. But You Must maintain Play Order [as in the Player that caused this Removal/Restoration Effect (either directly or indirectly) Must perform the above Shuffle & <a href="#glossary:deal">Re-Deal</a> of the Ancient Stones; and as the Dealer, they are last to receive one].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Special Victory Condition now Activates with by possessing all 4 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards in Play [Compare <a href="/01-a-crazy-8s-game-rulebook" class="matrix-link">(01-A) Soloverse 01-A Crazy 8's Game Rulebook: Cataclysm Expansion Additional Setup Rules</a>].</li>

<li>This Special Victory Condition now Activates with by possessing all 4 Ancient Stone Class Chaos Cards in Play [Compare <a href="/01-a-crazy-8s-game-rulebook" class="matrix-link">(01-A) Soloverse 01-A Crazy 8's Game Rulebook: Cataclysm Expansion Additional Setup Rules</a>].</li>
</ul>
</div>

</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-field-of-addition">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">FIELD OF ADDITION [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Field Deck Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Field Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Group Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/18-field-deck-1-field-of-addition.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>Every Number Card Played Must be Equal to or Higher than the Previous one Played.
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>0, 1, 1, 4, 9…</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>Any Action or Chaos Card Played are resolved as normal [e.g.: Choose a Colour to Continue after Resolving a Chaos Card], however it also Resets the Number Played, allowing any to be Played.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-field-of-deception">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">FIELD OF DECEPTION [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Field Deck Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Field Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Group Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/18-field-deck-7-field-of-deception.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>On your turn, You place Your Cards Face-Down instead, calling out what Cards you placed and how many there were, mimicking Soloverse [Cheat]’s Rules.
<div class="note-box">
<strong>NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>You do not have to tell the truth about the number of cards or their value.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>Cheating is allowed. But you will be penalised for getting caught.</li>
<li>At any point, any other Player Can call “Deception!” At which point the last Player who put down Must reveal their cards.
<ol type="a">
<li>If the Player was in fact Cheating, They Must now pick up the entire Discards.</li>
<li>If the Player was indeed telling the truth, the Challenging Player Must pick up the entire Discards instead!</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<h4 class="card-title">CHAOS DRAW +2 SPECIFIC TARGETED [x3]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Warzone Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Offensive Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Stackable</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Counterable</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Specific & Group Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/03-warzone-expansion-chaos-draw-2-specific-targeted.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Acts as Draw +2 & Pure Chaos.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li><a href="#glossary:targeted">Target</a> Any Player at the Table and They Must <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> 2 Cards and <a href="#glossary:miss-their-turn">Miss Their Turn</a> or <a href="#glossary:respond-in-kind">Respond in Kind</a>.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Choose a Colour to continue.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="compatibility-box">
<strong>COMPATIBILITY NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Can be <a href="#glossary:stackable">Stacked</a> with any Coloured <strong>"Draw +2"</strong>, <strong>"Chaos Draw +2"</strong> or <strong>"Chaos Draw +3"</strong>.</li>
<li>Can Also be <a href="#glossary:stackable">Stacked</a> with any other Specific Targeted Chaos Draw Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be played as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
<li>You Can only play this Card legally if You do NOT have a playable Coloured Card that matches what's in play, or by <a href="#glossary:respond-in-kind">Responding in Kind</a>.</li>
<div class="example-box">
<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>
<ul>
<li>The Card in Play is a Blue 2. The User Cannot possess any Blue or any Coloured 2 to legally play a <strong>"Chaos Draw +2 Specific Targeted"</strong>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="challenge-box">
<strong>CHALLENGE NOTES:</strong>
<ol>
<li>You May Challenge a Player when You think they have charged You <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> +2 while having a matching Coloured Card [excluding <a href="#glossary:respond-in-kind">Responding in Kind</a>].
<div class="note-box">
<strong>NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>You Can only Challenge or <a href="#glossary:respond-in-kind">Respond in Kind</a>… NOT both.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>The Challenged Player Must now show You Their entire Hand privately.</li>
<li>Once a Challenge is issued, the Player in the wrong will be charged an additional 1 Card on top of the 1 Card originally needed to be <a href="#glossary:draw">Drawn</a>:
<ol type="a">
<li>If the User was guilty [i.e.: they played it while possessing a matching Coloured Card], they Must <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> a total of 3 Cards instead of the Challenger.</li>
<li>If the User was innocent [i.e.: they played it without possessing a matching Coloured Card], the Challenger Must <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> a total of 3 Cards.</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</div>

<div class="compatibility-box">

<ul>

<li>You Can only play this Card legally if You do NOT have a playable Coloured Card that matches what's in play, or by <a href="#glossary:respond-in-kind">Responding in Kind</a>.

<strong>EXAMPLE:</strong>

<ol type="a">

<li>If the User was innocent [i.e.: they played it without possessing a matching Coloured Card], the Challenger Must <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> a total of 6 Cards.</li>

<li>You May Challenge a Player when You think they have charged You <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> +5 while having a matching Coloured Card [excluding <a href="#glossary:respond-in-kind">Responding in Kind</a>].
<div class="note-box">
<strong>NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>You Can only Challenge or <a href="#glossary:respond-in-kind">Respond in Kind</a>… NOT both.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>The Challenged Player Must now show You Their entire Hand privately.</li>
<li>Once a Challenge is issued, the Player in the wrong will be charged an additional 3 Cards on top of the 5 Cards originally needed to be <a href="#glossary:draw">Drawn</a>:
<ol type="a">
<li>If the User was guilty [i.e.: they played it while possessing a matching Coloured Card], they Must <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> a total of 9 Cards instead of the Challenger.</li>
<li>If the User was innocent [i.e.: they played it without possessing a matching Coloured Card], the Challenger Must <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> a total of 9 Cards.</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</div>

<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">ANTI-FUN [x3]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Core Game</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Counter Class LV:1</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Victory Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/00-core-game-counter-lv1-anti-fun.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Activate on Another Player's Turn to use this Card's Effect [but not if using it as a Pure Chaos Card (any Type) ].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>Choose One:
<ol type="a">
<li>Anti-Fun's Effect:
<ol type="i">
<li>On another Player’s Turn, when they place Their last Card to win, play this Card to Counter That Player's Win and force them to <a href="#glossary:deal">Deal</a> 5 New Cards; ending Their Turn.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>Or simply Play as a <a href="#crazy-8s-pure-chaos">Pure Chaos Card</a> [any Type].</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Play Order Continues Normally.</li>
<li>Choose a Colour to Continue.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Can be Pre-Emptively <a href="#glossary:counter">Countered</span></strong></a>, Officially, if <a href="#crazy-8s-nou">NOU</a> is Played Last.</li>
<li>But, there might be other, unofficial ways around it…</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-assassin">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">ASSASSIN [x3]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Doomsday Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Trap Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Instant Activation</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Self Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/15-doomsday-expansion-trap-assassin.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>When Revealed, You are Assassinated and Removed from the Game [“You lose! But there is hope...”].</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="note-box">
<strong>NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Should all but one Player be Removed From the Game, they Win.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Certain Chaos Cards have Their own 2 Player Game Note, and these Must be followed while only 2 Players are left due to being Removed from the Game [Example: <strong>"Reverse"</strong>, <strong>"Chaos Draw +4 Skip"</strong>, <strong>"Slap"</strong>, etc].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Can only be <a href="#glossary:counter">Countered</span></strong></a> by Bodyguard’s Effect.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol start="2">
<li>Only if You <a href="#glossary:counter">Countered</span></strong></a> it, continue to <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> if required.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Shuffle Assassin back into the Deck.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-badge-collector">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">BADGE COLLECTOR [x3]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Core Extension</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Normal Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Self Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/01-core-extension-badge-collector.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Play to the side of the Discards to use this Card's Effect [not if using it as a Pure Chaos Card (any Type) ].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>Choose one:
<ol type="a">
<li>Badge Collector's Effect:
<ol type="i">
<li>Discard a Minimum of 3 Differently Coloured Cards, no Maximum [one for any of the Coloured Sets].</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>Or simply Play as a <a href="#crazy-8s-pure-chaos">Pure Chaos Card</a> [any Type].</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Then place this Card on top of the Discards.</li>
<li>Choose a Colour to Continue.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-black-hole">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">BLACK HOLE [x2]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Core Extension</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Normal Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Group Targeting</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">User Goes Last</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/01-core-extension-black-hole.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Play to the side of the Discards to use this Card's Effect [not if using it as a Pure Chaos Card (any Type) ].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>Choose one:
<ol type="a">
<li>Black Hole's Effect:
<ol type="i">
<li>All Players in Play Order Discard all Their Hand Cards.</li>
<li>Then the User DEALS 5 Cards to Each Player.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>Or Play simply as a <a href="#crazy-8s-pure-chaos">Pure Chaos Card</a> [any Type].</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Then place this Card on top of the Discards.</li>
<li>Choose a Colour to Continue.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-black-knight">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">BLACK KNIGHT [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Oblivion Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Offensive & Royal Knight Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Specific Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/06-oblivion-expansion-black-knight.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<strong>ACTIVATION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Activate before You Start a <a href="#glossary:playable-turn">Playable Turn</span></strong></a>.</li>
<li>To use this Card's Effect, place it Face-Up on the table in front of You [as proof of ownership], resolve the Effects and then return it to Your Hand.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>Choose one:
<ol type="a">
<li>Black Knight's Usurper Effect:
<ol type="i">
<li>Choose any 2 Players to Swap Hands with [including Yourself].</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>Or Play simply as a <a href="#crazy-8s-pure-chaos">Pure Chaos Card</a> [any Type].</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class=note-box>
<strong>NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>The User maintains ownership of Black Knight.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1" start="2">
<li>Then continue Your Turn as normal.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class=restriction-box>
<strong>UNIQUE RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>This Effect Cannot affect the same Players two Activations in a row; regardless of who is the User and who is the <a href="#glossary:targeted">Target</a>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class=restriction-box>
<strong>CLASS RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be Your last Card.</li>
<li>Cannot be <a href="#glossary:counter">Countered</span></strong></a>, Except by King's Knight's Effect.</li>
<li>You Can only use one Royal Knight Effect per Turn, and it Can only be used once each Turn; however, You Can use it on additional Turns afterwards.</li>
<li>You Cannot use another Chaos Card on the same Turn You activate a Royal Knight Class Chaos Card; and vice versa.</li>
<li>You Cannot Go Out with this Card Effect.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="two-player-note-box">
<strong>2 PLAYER GAME NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Black Knight Can only <a href="#glossary:targeted">Target</a> the other Player every second Turn.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-bodyguard">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">BODYGUARD [x3]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Doomsday Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Counter Class LV:1</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Assassin Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/15-doomsday-expansion-counter-lv1-bodyguard.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>Choose one:
<ol type="a">
<li>When You are Self Targeted by an Assassin, play this Card to save Yourself.</li>
<li>If another Player is Self Targeted by an Assassin on Their Turn [before they are Removed From the Game], You Can:
<ol type="i">
<li>Offer Bodyguard as a private 1:1 trade to save Their life [if they have something to pay You with…].</li>
<li>Be a hero and give it to them for free!</li>
<li>Or simply ignore the situation.</li>
<li>The choice is Yours…</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>Instead of playing a Card on Your Turn, You Can choose to Shuffle Bodyguard back into the Deck on Your Turn.</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Play continues from the previous Card played.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="compatibility-box">
<strong>COMPATIBILITY NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>If You <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> Bodyguard and reveal an Assassin Trap Class Chaos Card in doing so, You May immediately Play it. This is a rare exception to the Pick Up/Put Down Rule for Chaos Cards [See: <a href="/01-a-crazy-8s-game-rulebook#pick-up-put-down" class="matrix-link">Crazy 8's Game Rulebook: How To Play: Rule 3: Note & Restriction]</a>.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be played as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-call-of-the-soloverse">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">CALL OF THE SOLOVERSE [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Singularity Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Trap Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Group Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/07-singularity-expansion-trap-call-of-the-soloverse.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>When Revealed, remove from the Deck Temporarily.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>When this Card is revealed, all Unification Class Chaos Cards in Player’s Hands Must be revealed to the group.</li>
<li>Optionally, if all 3 Unification Class Chaos Cards are NOT revealed, You Can choose to search the Deck for any Unification Class Chaos Card that is there and give it to any Player with the least amount of Unification Class Chaos Cards in Their Hand [including Yourself].</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>PENALTY NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>There are 3 Unification Class Chaos Cards in the Deck at the Start of the game. If the Deck does NOT contain the correct amount of Unification Class Chaos Cards when compared to the amount revealed, someone has played Theirs incorrectly – either by Discarding or NOT Revealing it.</li>
<li>If someone is found to have either Illegally Discarded [e.g.: Black Hole, Discard Chaos Cards] or NOT Revealed Their Unification Class Chaos Card [during the Effect of the Call of the Soloverse Trap Class Chaos Card], they Must Forfeit Their next 5 PLAYABLE TURNS.</li>
<li>Then Retrieve the Unification Class Chaos Card from the Discards and give it to a Player with the Least amount of Unification Class Chaos Cards in Their Hand.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol start="3">
<li>Continue to <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> if required.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Shuffle Call of the Soloverse back into the Deck.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol start="4">
<li>Play continues from the last Card on the Discards.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-clone">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">CLONE [x3]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Core Game</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Normal Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Stackable</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Counterable</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Unknown Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/00-core-game-clone.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>When played, it Copies the Previous Card played [be that a <strong>"Chaos Draw +4"</strong>, another Chaos Card or simply a Coloured Number or Action Class Card] as well as any Effects that Card might have.</li>
<li>Follow the rules for the previous Card.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot also be played as a Pure Chaos Card [Unless it is Cloned].</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="compatibility-box">
<strong>COMPATIBILITY NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>When copying another Clone Chaos Card, redirect to the first non-Clone Chaos Card on the Discards. Can even copy the Genetic Manipulation and ,Reverse Stun, Stun and Steal Turn Chaos Cards.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-contagion">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">CONTAGION [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Pandemic Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Trap Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Group Targeting</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">User Goes Last</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/11-pandemic-expansion-trap-05-contagion-all.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>When Revealed, remove from the Deck Temporarily.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>When Revealed, in Turn Order:
<ol type="a">
<li><strong>ALL OTHERS:</strong> <a href="#glossary:draw">Draws</a> 2 Cards.</li>
<li><strong>USER:</strong> <a href="#glossary:draw">Draws</a> 5 Cards.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>Continue to <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> if Required.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Shuffle Contagion back into the Deck.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="advanced-box">
<strong>COUNTERING NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Can only be <a href="#glossary:counter">Countered</span></strong></a> by <a href="#crazy-8s-cure">Cure's</a> Effect..</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-crisis">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">CRISIS [x?]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Crisis Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Crisis & Trap Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Self Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/08-crisis-expansion-trap-crisis.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>When revealed, place in front of You.</li>
<li>Continue to <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> if required.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="note-box">
<strong>NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>These Cards Cannot be Reshuffled back into the Deck.</li>
<li>These Cards Cannot be Swapped, Traded or Discarded.</li>
<li>These Cards do NOT count towards Your Hand Cards or <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw Effect</span></strong></a> Amount.</li>
<li>These Cards are Permanent for that game.</li>
<li>These Cards do NOT have any Effects except the Special Victory Conditions.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-cyclone">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">CYCLONE [x2]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Core Extension</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Normal Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Group Targeting</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">User Goes Last</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/01-core-extension-cyclone.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>The User Presents Their Hand Face-Down to the next Player in Play Order, who Chooses 1 Random Card from it and adds it to Their Hand.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="tip-box">
<strong>TIP:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Shuffling Your Hand before presenting it Face-Down [without looking at it Yourself] will stop Your Opponents from guessing which Card/s You want to keep.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1" start="2">
<li>That Player now Presents Their Hand to the next Player, Who Does the Same.</li>
<li>Continue until the User has <a href="#glossary:draw">Drawn</a> a Card from the previous Player.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Choose a Colour to continue.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be played as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
<li>Cannot be <a href="#glossary:counter">Countered</span></strong></a>.</li>
<li>Cannot be Your last Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-cure">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">CURE [x3]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Containment Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Counter Class LV:1</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Unknown Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/12-containment-expansion-cure.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol type="1">
<li>Choose one:
<ol type="a">
<li>Cancel the Activation of any Contagion Expansion Trap Class Chaos Card and Remove it from the Game.</li>
<li>Negate the <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw Effect</span></strong></a> charged against You [<a href="#glossary:stackable">Stacked</a> or otherwise].</li>
<li>On any <a href="#glossary:playable-turn">Playable Turn</span></strong></a>, You May surrender Cure by Shuffling it into the Deck instead of playing a Card.</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cure is always Shuffled back into the Deck and Cannot be Removed from the Game.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-discard-acid">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">DISCARD ACID [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Contingency I Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Trap Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Group Targeting</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">User Goes Last</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/13-contingency-i-expansion-trap-discard-acid.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Remove from the Deck Temporarily.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>All Players in Turn Order Discard all Acid [Orange] Cards.</li>
<li>Continue to <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> if required.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Shuffle Discard Acid back into the Deck.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol start="3">
<li>Play continues from the last Card Discarded by User.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-discard-amethyst">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">DISCARD AMETHYST [x1]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Contingency II Expansion</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Trap Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Group Targeting</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">User Goes Last</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/14-contingency-ii-expansion-trap-discard-amethyst.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Remove from the Deck Temporarily.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>All Players in Turn Order Discard all Amethyst [Light Purple] Cards.</li>
<li>Continue to <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> if required.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Shuffle Discard Amethyst back into the Deck.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol start="3">
<li>Play continues from the last Card Discarded by User.</li>
</ol>
</div>

<ol>
<li>Choose one:
<ol type="a">
<li>Discard Colour's Effect:
<ol type="i">

</ol>
</li>
<li>Or simply Play as a <a href="#crazy-8s-pure-chaos">Pure Chaos Card</a> [any Type].</li>

<div id="crazy-8s-double-edged">
<div class="rules-box">

<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">DOUBLE EDGED [x2]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Core Extension</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Offensive Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Self & Specific Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/01-core-extension-double-edged.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>Steal any Card in a Player’s Hand. Set it aside.</li>
<li>Then They Steal any Card in Your Hand and add it to Their Hand.</li>
<li>Add the Card You Stole to Your Hand.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Choose a Colour to continue.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="restriction-box">
<strong>RESTRICTION NOTES:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Cannot be played as a Pure Chaos Card.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="return-link-wrapper">
<a href="#crazy-8s-cards-contents" class="return-link">🔝 Return to 📋 RULEBOOK CONTENTS A-Z</a>
</div>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-double-under">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">DOUBLE UNDER [x2]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Core Extension</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Normal Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Self Targeting</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/01-core-extension-double-under.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>Choose one:
<ol type="a">
<li>Double Under's Effect:
<ol type="i">
<li>Return Your entire Hand to the Deck, Shuffle it and <a href="#glossary:deal">Deal</a> the same number of Cards to Yourself.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>Or simply Play as a <a href="#crazy-8s-pure-chaos">Pure Chaos Card</a> [any Type].</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>
<li>Choose a Colour to continue.</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div id="crazy-8s-elegant-generosity">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">

<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Core Game</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Normal Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Self Targeting</span></strong></li>

</div>

<ul>
<li>Play to the side of the Discards.</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>Choose one:
<ol type="a">
<li>Elegant Generosity's Effect:
<ol type="i">
<li>Discard Half Your remaining Cards [round up the Cards You keep] from Your Hand.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>Or simply Play as a <a href="#crazy-8s-pure-chaos">Pure Chaos Card</a> [any Type].</li>
</ol>
</li>

<div id="crazy-8s-eliminator">

<div class="card-top-section">

<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Core Extension</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Trap Class</span></strong></li>

</ul>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>

<li>In Play Order, all Players Discard all Coloured Cards that match that Number.</li>
<li>Continue to <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> if required.</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">

</ul>

<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Group Targeting</span></strong></li>

<div id="crazy-8s-foresight">
<div class="rules-box">
<div class="card-top-section">
<div class="rules-box">
<h4 class="card-title">FORESIGHT [x2]:</h4>
<ul>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Core Extension</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Trap Class</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">Group Targeting</span></strong></li>
<li><strong><span style="color: var(--color-card-class);">User Goes Last</span></strong></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="image-container">
<img src="/assets/images/cards/01-core-extension-trap-foresight.png">
</div>
</div>
<div class="rules-box">
<ol>
<li>All Players in Play Order Must guess the Colour of the next Face-Down Card [e.g.: any of the Coloured Sets in the Game such as Steel, Poison, Venom, Carbon, Stinger, Toxin or even Chaos (including Trap Class) ].
<ol type="a">
<li>If anyone guesses correctly, they Can now Discard 1 Card.</li>
<li>If anyone guesses incorrectly, they Must <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> 1 Card.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>Continue to <a href="#glossary:draw">Draw</span></strong></a> if required</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="discards-box">
<ul>

</ul>
</div>
